# Number check: Capstone report

Generated by `python docs/report/number_check.py`. Every number in the chapter text (`docs/report/src/*.md`, front matter, chapters and appendix) is looked up in the file named in the source comment that follows its block. "exact" means the same text occurs in the source; "rounded" means the source stores more digits and rounds to the report's value; "x100" means the source stores a fraction shown here as a percentage. Signs are compared by magnitude, and negative values were checked by hand. Dates come from `git log` and are not counted. Code excerpts are copied from the repository files named in their captions and are not counted.

**Total numbers: 5211. Matched: 5211. Unmatched or unsourced: 0.**

Blocks marked as formulas or structure (numcheck: ignore): 10 blocks, 23 tokens, listed at the end.
